# Complete source-file review ledger

Reviewed 28 October 2026. This ledger covers **2,288 source files**: **1,240 imported MIT files** and **1,048 restricted files inspected at their pinned upstream snapshot**. No restricted source code or prompt text is reproduced.

Read [CURRICULUM.md](CURRICULUM.md) for the complete 427-entry teaching sequence, licensing limits and contract findings. Hashes below identify exact source bytes; hashes do not prove manual reading.

## Method key

| Code | Inspection |
| --- | --- |
| T | Authored text read in full. |
| N | Authored text read with the exercise function identifier replaced by a reversible FUNCTION placeholder to reduce repetitive presentation; the original name is recorded in the source path/presentation. This does not mean an unseen implementation was inferred from its title. |
| I | Compared with previously read identical text, sometimes after the documented identifier normalization. |
| D | Differences read against a previously read base file. |
| L | Generated lockfile parsed completely; structure, compiler versions and catalogue inspected. Individual dependency records were not all manually read. |
| X | Authored README prose read; generated index navigation checked against all 190 challenge directories, not every repeated badge byte manually read. |
| V | Raster image decoded and visually inspected in a contact sheet; dimensions/hash checked. |
| S | SVG element structure and text inspected as branding vector data. |

For I/D entries, the base path is shown below the file tables. N/I normalization only substitutes the task function name; it does not discard implementation differences. Batch numbers identify reading groups, not source task numbers. Two early batches share the number 0 because the presentation was restarted; notes are retained separately. “Special” denotes the final generated-data/asset/script pass. The earlier structural inventory in curriculum/file-review.json is preservation evidence, not a substitute for these reading methods.

## Detailed reading notes

These notes preserve the observations from each reading group. They are audit notes, so source IDs and abbreviated notation are retained. The ordered sequence presents the material learner-facing findings beside their tasks. Earlier “pending” references describe the state of that particular reading group; later groups and the complete ledger above close those items.

### Reading group 01 (batch 0; 33 files)

Read setup, source attribution and EN/ES welcome pages; exercises 001–003 are scalar threshold predicates; 004 deliberately always false. Read all displayed starter/runtime/reference/test contents; 004 solution/tests remain pending.

### Reading group 02 (batch 0; 50 files)

Reviewed every displayed file from 004 solution through 012 tests. New blocker: 005 TypeScript starter returns boolean despite string contract in both prompts, tests, and JS reference; 012 folder says isLessThanThirty but symbol is isLessThan30. Scalar string/number predicates are easier than rest callbacks and should precede them.

### Reading group 03 (batch 1; 53 files)

Read 013–020 completely and 021 through solution. Comparison operand order is reversed from common naming in 014/015; 016 tests reject numeric strings despite numeric annotations. Odd references use remainder >0 and do not cover negative integers; preserve integer/negative assumptions. Move single comparisons ahead of combined parity predicates.

### Reading group 04 (batch 2; 58 files)

Reviewed 021 tests through 031 JS starter. 025 average should precede 023 average string lengths; square is simpler than cube and area/perimeter combinations. Simple formula tasks before callback/rest-function lesson. All bilingual prompts and associated tested boundaries read.

### Reading group 05 (batch 3; 49 files)

Read 031 TS onwards through 039 starter. Confirmed composition order: rectangle area before tripled area, square root before doubled square root, parity predicates before compound OR/AND. Circle tests use exact Math.PI floating outputs. 039 solution/tests pending.

### Reading group 06 (batch 4; 45 files)

Read 039 solution/test through 047 ES prompt. 040 average arrays is misplaced ahead of basic indexing and summation; move after array sums. 041 bans length and needs traversal, unlike 007. 042 tests presence of console.log but not exact badge string. Indexing tests include undefined for empty arrays.

### Reading group 07 (batch 5; 46 files)

Read 047 EN through 054 solution. Mutation and immutable removal are distinct lessons; 053 tests original unchanged, 051/052 only output. 049/050 starters incorrectly annotate numeric input arrays and string outputs despite string-array test examples; list prerequisite normalization blockers.

### Reading group 08 (batch 6; 42 files)

Read 054 tests through 061 solution. OR exercise forbids || and asks De Morgan; place after Boolean operators. 059 extend belongs with object mutations, not early array operations. 060 split/join replacement after strings/arrays. Source 055 wording says three a characters in I am a hacker but actual string has three including hacker; correct. Pending 061 tests.

Read all 061 tests–068 tests. 062 forbids indexOf (tests only results). 063/064 scalar minima/maxima easier than array extrema and should precede them. 067 test supplies four arguments despite declared three; distinguish JS fixture permissiveness from TS contract. 065 repeat basic string library task should move earlier.

Pair mutate/immutable variants for progression. keep/remove then parity-word filters; generic unknown-array deletion no generic relation retained in starter.
